<script>
/* 库存统一读完整主表；YQN细仓/库龄保留独立日期和数量含义。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  if (root.inventoryConsistencyModel?.ready) return;
  const node = document.getElementById('inventoryConsistencyData');
  if (!node) throw new Error('统一库存数据未载入');
  const data = JSON.parse(node.textContent), active = new Map(data.rows.map(row => [row.sku,row]));
  const master = new Map(data.allMasterRows.map(row => [row.sku,row]));
  const date = data.meta.sourceSnapshotDate, label = data.meta.sourceSnapshotLabel;
  const number = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const q = value => number(value) ? cxQty(value) : '待核';
  const esc = value => cxEsc(value == null ? '' : value);
  function current(sku) {
    const key=data.aliases?.[sku] || sku;
    return active.get(key) || master.get(key) || {sku,available:null,reserved:null,transit:null,masterRegions:{},reason:'最新完整主表未登记此精确SKU，保留未知，不用历史0代替当前余额。'};
  }
  function apply(target, source) {
    if (!target) return;
    for (const key of ['available','reserved','transit']) target[key] = source[key];
    target.stock_date = label + '（业务截止未单独声明）';
    target.masterRegions = source.masterRegions;
    target.primaryInventoryEvidence = source;
    target.stockAuthority = '完整主表已登记仓区合计，不叠加YQN';
    target.stock_source = Object.values(source.masterRegions).map(value => value.source.file+' / '+value.source.sheet+' / 第'+value.source.row+'行').join('；') || source.reason;
    target.regions = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(source.masterRegions).map(([key,value]) => [key,{available_sets:value.available,reserved_sets:value.reserved,in_transit_sets:value.transit,in_transit_reserved_sets:value.transitReserved,source_sheet:value.source.sheet,source_row:value.source.row}]));
  }
  for (const map of [typeof cxStockMap !== 'undefined' && cxStockMap, typeof sqStockBySku !== 'undefined' && sqStockBySku]) {
    if (map) for (const [sku,row] of map) apply(row,current(sku));
  }
  if (typeof sqStockRecord === 'function') {
    const before = sqStockRecord;
    sqStockRecord = function (sku) { const row = before(sku); apply(row,current(sku)); return row; };
  }
  if (typeof rwData !== 'undefined' && rwData?.rows) {
    for (const row of rwData.rows) {
      const source = current(row.sku);
      row.defaultVisible = active.has(row.sku);
      row.masterRegions = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(source.masterRegions).map(([region,value]) => [region,{...value,sourceSheet:value.source.sheet,sourceRow:value.source.row,date:'文件保存'+date}]));
      row.primaryInventoryEvidence = source;
    }
    rwData.meta.masterDate = '文件保存'+date;
    rwData.meta.masterMeaning = data.meta.nationalBasis+' '+data.meta.sourceDateBasis;
  }
  if (root.spInventoryModel?.ready) {
    root.spInventoryModel.data.meta = data.meta;
    root.spInventoryModel.data.rows = data.rows;
    root.spInventoryModel.data.summary = data.summary;
    root.spInventoryModel.bySku.clear();
    for (const row of data.rows) root.spInventoryModel.bySku.set(row.sku,row);
  }
  // 新品“出单监控”展示当前余额；np七个月补货方案的历史测算输入不改。
  if(typeof nmData!=='undefined'&&nmData?.rows){
    for(const row of nmData.rows){
      const source=current(row.inventorySku||row.sku);
      for(const key of ['available','reserved','transit'])row[key]=source[key];
      row.stockDate=label+'（业务截止未单独声明）';row.currentInventoryFileDate=date;
      row.primaryInventoryEvidence=source;
      if(typeof nmBySku!=='undefined')nmBySku.set(row.sku,row);
    }
    nmData.meta.stockReadDate='文件保存'+date;
    nmData.meta.stockDateBasis=data.meta.sourceDateBasis;
  }
  function clean(html) {
    return String(html).replaceAll('美国站库存主表 · 文件保存2026-11-13',label)
      .replaceAll('文件保存2026-11-13','文件保存'+date).replaceAll('文件11/13','文件'+date.slice(5).replace('-','/'))
      .replaceAll('文件保存11/13','文件保存'+date.slice(5).replace('-','/'))
      .replaceAll('主表 · 11/11','主表 · 文件'+date.slice(5).replace('-','/'))
      .replaceAll('三区主表11/11','三区主表 · 文件'+date.slice(5).replace('-','/'))
      .replaceAll('全国去重快照','当前完整主表').replaceAll('原全国可用','全国主表可用');
  }
  for (const name of ['cxSkuStockHtml','sqStockHtml','ldStockCharts']) {
    // 这些旧函数返回HTML；只修正主表日期文案，YQN原日期保留。
    if (name === 'cxSkuStockHtml' && typeof cxSkuStockHtml === 'function') { const before = cxSkuStockHtml; cxSkuStockHtml = function () { return clean(before.apply(this,arguments)); }; }
    if (name === 'sqStockHtml' && typeof sqStockHtml === 'function') { const before = sqStockHtml; sqStockHtml = function () { return clean(before.apply(this,arguments)); }; }
    if (name === 'ldStockCharts' && typeof ldStockCharts === 'function') { const before = ldStockCharts; ldStockCharts = function () { const value = before.apply(this,arguments); return {...value,html:clean(value.html)}; }; }
  }
  const planInventoryLabel='方案库存快照（11/12）';
  function planLabels(html){return String(html).replaceAll('当前可用',planInventoryLabel+'可用').replaceAll('当前在途',planInventoryLabel+'在途').replaceAll('新品当前库存读取11/12','新品方案库存快照读取11/12');}
  if(typeof npSummary==='function'){const before=npSummary;npSummary=function(){return planLabels(before.apply(this,arguments));};}
  if(typeof npLatestDetail==='function'){const before=npLatestDetail;npLatestDetail=function(){return planLabels(before.apply(this,arguments));};}
  if(typeof npStockDetail==='function'){const before=npStockDetail;npStockDetail=function(){return planLabels(before.apply(this,arguments));};}
  if(typeof cxSkuOverview==='function'){const before=cxSkuOverview;cxSkuOverview=function(sku){const html=before.apply(this,arguments);return typeof npMap!=='undefined'&&npMap.has(sku)?planLabels(html):html;};}
  if(typeof npRenderNews==='function'){
    const before=npRenderNews;
    npRenderNews=function(){const value=before.apply(this,arguments),host=cxEl('cxNewsBody');if(host)host.innerHTML=planLabels(host.innerHTML);return value;};
    cxRenderNews=function(){return nmState.mode==='monitor'?nmRender():npRenderNews();};
  }
  function cohort() { return wlFilteredRows(); }
  function ageItems(rows) {
    return sqAgeBands.map(key => ({key,label:key,value:wlAgeTotal(rows,key)}));
  }
  if (typeof wlAgeChart === 'function') wlAgeChart = function () {
    const rows = cohort(), selected = cxState.stock.age;
    return '<section class="cx-chart wl-age-chart"><h3>命中'+rows.length+'款SKU · 全国全龄段物理套</h3>'+cxBarChart(ageItems(rows),'data-cx-stock-age',selected)
      +'<p class="cx-note ici-source">YQN库龄快照 '+esc(rwData.meta.ageDate || '2026-11-11')+'，含预留。与本页卡片、仓库和明细使用同一批命中SKU；保留这些SKU的其他入库批次。'+(selected?'选中'+esc(selected)+'为'+q(wlAgeTotal(rows,selected))+'物理套；其他柱是同批SKU的其他库龄。':'点击龄段筛含该龄段的SKU。')+' 不能与主表可用库存相加。</p></section>';
  };
  if (typeof wlAgeExplanation === 'function') wlAgeExplanation = function (rows) {
    const band = cxState.stock.age;
    if (!band) return '';
    return '<div class="wl-age-explanation ici-explanation"><strong>'+esc(band)+' · 命中'+rows.length+'款SKU</strong><span>该龄段：<b data-wl-age-total>'+q(wlAgeTotal(rows,band))+'物理套</b>（YQN '+esc(rwData.meta.ageDate || '2026-11-11')+'，含预留）。</span><span>主表卡片和分仓表展示这'+rows.length+'款SKU的<b>全龄段</b>数量；“可用”已排除不可用部分，“物理”包含预留，来源日期也不同。</span><button class="btn" data-wl-clear-age>取消库龄点选</button></div>';
  };
  if (typeof rwCards === 'function') { const before=rwCards; rwCards=function(rows){return clean(before(rows)).replaceAll('<small>可用</small>','<small>全龄可用</small>').replaceAll('<small>预留</small>','<small>全龄预留</small>').replaceAll('<small>在途</small>','<small>在途登记</small>');}; }
  if (typeof wlWarehouseHtml === 'function') { const before=wlWarehouseHtml; wlWarehouseHtml=function(rows){return before(rows).replace('具体仓库</h3>','具体仓库 · 命中SKU全龄段</h3>').replace('物理已知小计','物理已知小计（含预留）');}; rwWarehouseHtml=wlWarehouseHtml; }
  if (typeof rwTableHtml === 'function') { const before=rwTableHtml; rwTableHtml=function(rows){return clean(before(rows));}; }
  if (typeof wlStatusChart === 'function') wlStatusChart = function () {
    const rows=cohort(),aggregate=wlAggregate(rows),items=[['available','全龄可用'],['reserved','全龄预留'],['transit','在途登记']].map(([key,text])=>({key,text,value:aggregate[key],count:rows.filter(row=>(wlKnownForRow(row,key)||0)>0).length})),max=Math.max(1,...items.map(item=>number(item.value)?item.value:0));
    return '<section class="cx-chart wl-status-chart"><div class="rw-section-title"><h3>同批SKU · 库存与供给状态</h3><span>'+esc(rwState.basis==='master'&&!rwState.warehouse?label:'分仓独立旁证')+'</span></div><div class="wl-status-bars">'+items.map((item,index)=>'<button class="wl-status-bar '+(wlState.status===item.key?'is-active':'')+'" data-wl-status="'+item.key+'" aria-pressed="'+(wlState.status===item.key)+'"><span>'+item.text+'<small>'+item.count+'款SKU有正量</small></span><span class="wl-status-track"><i style="width:'+(number(item.value)?100*item.value/max:0)+'%;background:'+['#4b709f','#8a7da7','#b38853'][index]+'"></i></span><b>'+q(item.value)+'<small>套 · 已知小计</small></b></button>').join('')+'</div><p class="cx-note">与上方命中SKU及明细同一人群。预留不再加到可用；在途不计现货。库存来源缺项保留待核。</p></section>';
  };
  if (typeof rwReconciliation === 'function') rwReconciliation = function (rows) {
    const a=rwMasterAggregate(rows),warehouses=rows.flatMap(row=>row.warehouses.filter(rwPresent)),external=warehouses.filter(row=>row.type==='external'),sum=key=>external.reduce((total,row)=>total+rwKnown(row,key),0);
    return '<details class="cx-fold rw-reconcile"><summary>来源对账 · 当前主表与独立分仓旁证</summary><div class="cx-fold-body">'+cxTable(['来源及人群','日期','数量','含义'],[
      ['完整三区主表 · '+rows.length+'款',label,'可用 '+q(a.available)+' / 预留 '+q(a.reserved)+' / 在途 '+q(a.transit),'同源已登记仓区合计；未列仓区不补0'],
      ['YQN外仓 · 同批SKU全龄段',rwData.meta.externalDate,'可用 '+q(sum('available'))+' / 预留 '+q(sum('reserved'))+' / 物理 '+q(sum('physical')),'物理包含预留；独立旧快照，不加到当前主表'],
      ['YQN库龄 · 同批SKU',rwData.meta.ageDate,'全龄物理 '+q(wlAgeTotal(rows,''))+(cxState.stock.age?' / '+cxState.stock.age+' '+q(wlAgeTotal(rows)) : ''),'全国龄段不等于所选仓内龄段；未取得最新库龄不伪造更新']
    ])+'<p class="cx-note">不同日期与“可用 / 含预留物理 / 单一龄段”不可直接相减判断丢件。本版只统一主表余额；细仓和库龄保留其原始日期。</p><p class="cx-source">'+esc(data.meta.primarySource.file)+' · SHA256 '+esc(data.meta.primarySource.sha256)+'</p></div></details>';
  };
  if (typeof wlRender === 'function') wlRender = function () {
    const host=cxEl('cxStockBody');if(!host)return;
    const rows=rwSorted(cohort()),aggregate=wlAggregate(rows),isMaster=rwState.basis==='master'&&!rwState.warehouse,s=cxState.stock;
    host.innerHTML='<div class="cx-card"><div class="cx-controls"><input id="rwStockSearch" placeholder="搜索SKU、仓库、中文区域" value="'+esc(s.search)+'" aria-label="库存SKU或仓库搜索"><label>范围<select id="rwStockScope"><option value="active">当前关注SKU</option><option value="all">包含历史身份</option></select></label><label>展示来源<select id="rwBasis"><option value="master">完整三区主表 · 文件'+esc(date)+'</option><option value="warehouse">YQN/自有分仓 · 独立旧快照</option></select></label><button class="btn" data-rw-reset>恢复全部</button><button class="btn" data-rw-export>导出当前库存明细CSV</button></div><div class="rw-stats">'+rwMetric('命中SKU',rows.length)+rwMetric('全龄可用 · '+(isMaster?'主表':'分仓小计'),aggregate.available)+rwMetric('全龄预留',aggregate.reserved)+rwMetric('在途登记',aggregate.transit)+'</div><p class="ici-source">'+esc(label)+'；业务截止未单独声明。YQN分仓 '+esc(rwData.meta.externalDate)+'、库龄 '+esc(rwData.meta.ageDate)+' 为独立旁证。三处数量不相加。</p>'+wlAgeExplanation(rows)+'</div>'+rwCards(rows)+'<div class="rw-focus">'+esc([rwState.region&&rwLabels[rwState.region],rwState.warehouse,cxState.stock.age,wlState.status&&wlStatusLabels[wlState.status]].filter(Boolean).join(' / ')||'全部仓区')+(rwState.region||rwState.warehouse?'<button class="btn" data-rw-clear>取消仓区/仓库</button>':'')+(wlState.status?'<button class="btn" data-wl-clear-status>取消状态</button>':'')+'</div><div class="cx-charts two rw-detail-charts wl-detail-charts">'+wlWarehouseHtml(rows)+'<div class="wl-right-charts">'+wlAgeChart()+wlStatusChart()+'</div></div>'+rwTableHtml(rows)+rwReconciliation(rows);
    cxEl('rwStockScope').value=s.scope;cxEl('rwBasis').value=rwState.basis;
  };
  if(typeof wlRender==='function'){rwRender=wlRender;cxRenderStock=wlRender;}
  if(typeof wlExport==='function') {
    const before=wlExport;
    wlExport=function(){return before.apply(this,arguments);};rwExport=wlExport;cxExportStock=wlExport;
  }
  function refreshMarkdown() {
    const m=root.markdownFollowupModel;if(!m?.ready)return;
    m.meta.strategyInventoryDate=m.meta.strategyInventoryDate||m.meta.inventoryDate;
    m.meta.inventoryDate=date;m.meta.inventoryDateBasis='完整主表文件日期，策略旧批数量沿原复核快照';
    for(const row of m.rows){
      if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(row,'strategyAvailable'))row.strategyAvailable=row.available;
      if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(row,'strategyRemainingDays'))row.strategyRemainingDays=row.remainingDays;
      row.available=current(row.sku).available;row.currentInventoryFileDate=date;
      const exact30=number(row.net30Lower)&&number(row.net30Upper)&&row.net30Lower===row.net30Upper;
      const exact60=number(row.net60Lower)&&number(row.net60Upper)&&row.net60Lower===row.net60Upper;
      const window=exact30&&row.net30Lower>0?30:exact30&&row.net30Lower===0&&exact60&&row.net60Lower>0?60:null;
      row.remainingDays=number(row.available)&&window?row.available*window/(window===30?row.net30Lower:row.net60Lower):null;
      row.selloutGroup=number(row.remainingDays)?row.remainingDays>=90&&row.remainingDays<=120?'3–4个月':row.remainingDays>120?'4个月以上':'不足3个月':!exact30||!exact60?'净量待核':'零动销，无法有限预测';
      row.currentSelloutBasis='当前主表文件'+date+'；'+(window?'采用已核'+window+'日净量':'缺少可用的精确正量窗口，未生成有限售罄天数');
    }
    return m;
  }
  function decorateMarkdown() {
    const m=root.markdownFollowupModel;if(!m?.ready)return;
    const dates=document.getElementById('mdrDates');
    if(dates&&!dates.textContent.includes('策略库存快照'))dates.textContent+=' · 策略库存快照'+m.meta.strategyInventoryDate+'（原旧批执行范围保留）';
    const title=document.getElementById('mdrDetailTitle'),body=document.getElementById('mdrDetailBody');
    const sku=title?.textContent.split(' · ')[0],row=m.bySku?.get(sku);
    if(body&&row&&!body.querySelector('.ici-strategy-reference'))body.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin','<div class="ici-source ici-strategy-reference">当前可用：'+q(row.available)+'套（主表文件'+esc(date)+'）；策略原可用：'+q(row.strategyAvailable)+'套（'+esc(m.meta.strategyInventoryDate)+'）。旧批降价范围沿原复核证据，未把当前全量自动转为执行数量。当前售罄参考：'+(number(row.remainingDays)?q(row.remainingDays)+'天':esc(row.selloutGroup))+'；'+esc(row.currentSelloutBasis)+'</div>');
  }
  if (root.markdownFollowupModel?.ready) {
    const m=refreshMarkdown();
    m.render();
    decorateMarkdown();
    const beforeRender=m.render;m.render=function(){const value=beforeRender.apply(this,arguments);decorateMarkdown();return value;};
    const beforeDetail=m.openDetail;if(beforeDetail)m.openDetail=function(){const value=beforeDetail.apply(this,arguments);decorateMarkdown();return value;};
    document.addEventListener('click',event=>{if(event.target.closest?.('[data-mdr-sku],[data-mdr-queue],[data-mdr-close]')&&typeof queueMicrotask==='function')queueMicrotask(decorateMarkdown);},true);
    document.addEventListener('input',event=>{if(event.target.id==='mdrSearch'&&typeof queueMicrotask==='function')queueMicrotask(decorateMarkdown);},true);
    document.addEventListener('change',event=>{if(event.target.id==='mdrColor'&&typeof queueMicrotask==='function')queueMicrotask(decorateMarkdown);},true);
  }
  if (typeof sqActiveRows !== 'undefined') for (const row of sqActiveRows) {
    if (row.stockClass === 'nonstock') continue;
    const source=current(row.sku);for(const key of ['available','reserved','transit'])row[key]=source[key];row.stockDate=label;
  }
  if(typeof sqSalesCache!=='undefined')sqSalesCache.clear();
  root.spQueryPeriodModel?.clearCaches?.();
  if(typeof ldReferenceCache!=='undefined')ldReferenceCache.clear();
  if(typeof cxDownloadCsv==='function'){
    const before=cxDownloadCsv;
    cxDownloadCsv=function(filename,headers,rows){
      if(/^新品出单监控_/.test(filename)){
        headers=[...headers,'库存主表文件日期','库存日期含义'];
        rows=rows.map(row=>[...row,date,'完整主表文件日期；业务结算截止未单独声明']);
      }
      if(/^新品_最新销量与拟定滚动补货_/.test(filename))headers=headers.map(h=>h==='当前可用'?planInventoryLabel+'可用':h==='当前在途'?planInventoryLabel+'在途':h);
      if(/^库存(?:与在途|分区分仓)_/.test(filename)){
        // 保留ISO主表日期尾缀，避免旧下载装饰器把报表日再次改回11/16。
        filename=filename.replace(/20\d{6}(?=\.csv$)/,'20261118_主表'+date);
        const index=headers.indexOf('未知处理');
        headers=headers.map(h=>h==='原全国去重可用'?'当前全国主表可用':h);
        rows=rows.map(row=>{const value=row.slice();if(index>=0)value[index]='完整主表文件'+date+'；YQN分仓11/12、库龄11/11独立；所选龄段筛SKU，其他库存为这些SKU全龄段；缺行保留未知，不相加';return value;});
      }
      return before(filename,headers,rows);
    };
  }
  root.inventoryConsistencyModel={ready:true,data,active,master,current,cohort,ageItems,clean,refreshMarkdown,decorateMarkdown};
  if(cxState.view==='warehouses')cxRenderStock();
  const marker=document.createElement('span');marker.id='inventoryConsistencyReady';marker.hidden=true;document.body.appendChild(marker);
})(window);

/* 费用构成复用同SKU利润样本；不重新取样，不改变任何利润或销量口径。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const profitModel = root.ppProfitModel;
  if (!profitModel?.ready) throw Error('费用构成需要同SKU利润模型先就绪');
  const finite = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const esc = value => cxEsc(String(value ?? ''));
  const money = value => finite(value) ? cxMoney(value) : '未取得';
  const pct = value => finite(value) ? (value * 100).toFixed(2) + '%' : '未取得';
  const fields = [ ['head','头程'], ['tariff','关税'], ['handling','仓库作业'], ['tail','尾程'],
    ['platformFee','平台费'], ['advertising','广告'], ['consumptionTax','消费税'],
    ['installation','安装'] ];
  const ui = { basis: 'operating', storage: false };
  const activeSku = () => cxState.detail?.sku || (cxState.view === 'query' && typeof sqDetailRow !== 'undefined' ? sqDetailRow?.sku : '');
  function currentFilters() {
    if (cxState.view === 'query') return { ...sqUiState.filters };
    if (typeof cfReferenceScope === 'function') return { ...cfReferenceScope(), evidenceMode: cfState.profitMode === 'current' ? 'current' : 'withHistory' };
    return { platform: cxState.sales?.platform || '', store: typeof sxState !== 'undefined' ? sxState.store || '' : '', evidenceMode: 'current' };
  }
  function forSku(sku, filters = {}, options = {}) {
    const result = profitModel.forSku(sku, filters), summary = result.selected;
    const samples = result.samples || [], basis = options.basis === 'true' ? 'true' : 'operating';
    const wantedStorage = Boolean(options.storage), missing = [];
    if (!summary || !samples.length) return { sku, basis, samples: [], sampleCount: 0, complete: false, missing: result.ownBackfillEvidence?.missingFields || ['没有此SKU自身完整订单利润样本'], result };
    // 源商品码可保留已核WKC/EKC等别名；只复用利润模型已确认的库存SKU与同一批sampleKeys。
    // 不在展示层重新剥后缀、扩大匹配或否定上游显式映射。
    const keysMatch = samples.length === summary.sampleCount && samples.every((sample, i) => sample.key === summary.sampleKeys[i] && sample.sku === sku);
    if (!keysMatch) missing.push('费用样本与主列利润样本不一致');
    const sum = getter => {
      const values = samples.map(getter);
      return values.every(finite) ? values.reduce((total, value) => total + value, 0) : null;
    };
    const revenue = sum(sample => sample.revenue), quantity = sum(sample => sample.quantity);
    const storageKnown = samples.filter(sample => finite(sample.warehouseFeeTotal)
      && finite(sample.storageOperatingProfitTotal) && finite(sample.storageTrueProfitTotal));
    const storageComplete = storageKnown.length === samples.length;
    // 仓储未齐时保留纯利润分母，不能把已知8单仓租扣到20单收入中。
    const storage = wantedStorage && storageComplete;
    const components = [{ key: 'purchase', label: basis === 'true' ? '真实采购' : '运营采购',
      amount: sum(sample => sample[basis === 'true' ? 'truePurchase' : 'operatingPurchase'] * sample.quantity) },
      ...fields.map(([key, label]) => ({ key, label, amount: sum(sample => sample.componentTotals?.[key]) }))];
    if (storage) components.push({ key: 'storage', label: '订单仓储费', amount: sum(sample => sample.warehouseFeeTotal) });
    for (const component of components) {
      component.share = finite(revenue) && revenue > 0 && finite(component.amount) ? component.amount / revenue : null;
      if (!finite(component.amount)) missing.push(component.label + '存在缺项');
    }
    const profitField = storage ? (basis === 'true' ? 'storageTrueProfitTotal' : 'storageOperatingProfitTotal')
      : (basis === 'true' ? 'pureTrueProfitTotal' : 'pureOperatingProfitTotal');
    const profit = sum(sample => sample[profitField]);
    const costTotal = components.every(row => finite(row.amount)) ? components.reduce((total, row) => total + row.amount, 0) : null;
    const conservationDelta = finite(costTotal) && finite(profit) && finite(revenue) ? revenue - costTotal - profit : null;
    if (!finite(conservationDelta) || Math.abs(conservationDelta) > 0.02) missing.push('费用合计与现有利润未闭合');
    if (!(finite(revenue) && revenue > 0)) missing.push('订单收入分母缺失');
    return { sku, basis, storage, wantedStorage, storageComplete, storageSampleCount: storageKnown.length,
      samples, sampleCount: samples.length, sampleKeys: samples.map(sample => sample.key), summary,
      revenue, quantity, components, profit, profitShare: finite(revenue) && revenue > 0 ? profit / revenue : null,
      costTotal, costShare: finite(revenue) && revenue > 0 && finite(costTotal) ? costTotal / revenue : null,
      conservationDelta, modelReserve: sum(sample => sample.modelAftersalesReserve), complete: !missing.length, missing, result };
  }
  function scopeText(value) {
    const s = value.summary;
    return s.periodStart + '—' + s.periodEnd + ' · 最近' + value.sampleCount + '笔有效订单 · '
      + s.platformCount + '平台／' + s.storeCount + '店铺 · ' + s.stores.join('、').replace(/Shopify/g, '独立站');
  }
  function render(sku, filters = currentFilters(), options = ui) {
    const value = forSku(sku, filters, options);
    if (!value.sampleCount) return '<section class="cc-cost-composition pp-section" data-costs-ready="true" data-costs-sku="' + esc(sku)
      + '"><h3>费用金额及占收入比</h3><p>没有此SKU自身完整成本样本，暂不能计算费用占比。</p><p class="cc-note">'
      + esc(value.missing.join('；')) + '。不使用其他SKU成本，不把缺项当0。</p></section>';
    const button = (key, selected, label, disabled = false) => '<button type="button" data-cc-action="' + key
      + '" aria-pressed="' + selected + '" class="' + (selected ? 'active' : '') + '"' + (disabled ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + label + '</button>';
    const rows = value.components.map(row => '<tr><th scope="row">' + esc(row.label) + '</th><td>' + money(row.amount)
      + '</td><td>' + pct(row.share) + '</td><td><span class="cc-track"><span style="width:'
      + (finite(row.share) ? Math.min(100, Math.abs(row.share * 100)).toFixed(4) : 0) + '%"></span></span></td></tr>').join('');
    return '<section class="cc-cost-composition pp-section" data-costs-ready="true" data-costs-sku="' + esc(sku)
      + '"><div class="cc-heading"><h3>费用金额及占收入比</h3><button type="button" data-cc-action="export">导出费用构成CSV</button></div>'
      + '<p>' + esc(scopeText(value)) + '。</p><p class="cc-note">与上方利润主列使用同一批样本；销量日期不截断历史成本。'
      + (value.result.isForecast ? '当前为通知价下的预估收入和费用，非改价后实际结算。' : '历史原成交收入与原单费用模型。') + '</p>'
      + '<div class="cc-controls" aria-label="费用口径">' + button('operating', value.basis === 'operating', '运营采购口径')
      + button('true', value.basis === 'true', '真实采购口径') + '<span class="cc-divider"></span>'
      + button('pure', !value.storage, '未含累计仓储') + button('storage', value.storage, '含仓储毛利', !value.storageComplete) + '</div>'
      + '<div class="cc-totals"><div><span>样本收入 · 占比统一分母</span><strong>' + money(value.revenue) + '</strong></div>'
      + '<div><span>费用合计</span><strong>' + money(value.costTotal) + ' <small>' + pct(value.costShare) + '</small></strong></div>'
      + '<div><span>' + (value.basis === 'true' ? '真实' : '运营') + (value.storage ? '毛利 · 含订单仓储' : '利润 · 未含累计仓储') + '</span><strong class="' + (value.profit < 0 ? 'loss' : '') + '">' + money(value.profit) + ' <small>' + pct(value.profitShare) + '</small></strong></div></div>'
      + '<div class="cc-table-wrap"><table class="cc-table"><thead><tr><th>费用项目</th><th>金额 USD</th><th>占样本收入</th><th aria-label="占比示意">占比示意</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + rows + '</tbody></table></div>'
      + '<p class="cc-note">每项占比＝费用金额÷同批样本收入；费用合计＋利润＝收入。亏损时费用占比可超过100%，条形最长显示100%，数值保留实际占比。运营采购＝真实采购×1.5，其余费用共用原单金额。</p>'
      + '<p class="cc-note">仓储费用 '+value.storageSampleCount+'/'+value.sampleCount+'笔可算。'
      + (value.storageComplete ? '可切换含仓储毛利。订单页区分结算实据和按订单日库龄测算。'
        : '全样本毛利待核。逐单表显示已核费用、同仓参考和缺证原因。') + '</p>'
      + (finite(value.modelReserve) ? '<p class="cc-note">原模型售后预留 '+money(value.modelReserve)+' · 未扣入费用和利润。</p>' : '')
      + (!value.complete ? '<p class="cc-warning">' + esc(value.missing.join('；')) + '；当前费用构成待核，不替换现有利润。</p>' : '')
      + '<button type="button" data-cc-action="samples">查看同批逐单费用与原凭证 ↓</button></section>';
  }
  function csvRows(value) {
    if (!value.sampleCount) return [];
    const common = [value.sku, value.basis === 'true' ? '真实采购' : '运营采购', value.storage ? '含订单仓储毛利' : '未含累计仓储',
      value.summary.periodStart, value.summary.periodEnd, value.sampleCount, value.summary.platformCount, value.summary.storeCount,
      value.summary.stores.join('；'), value.revenue];
    const rows = value.components.map(row => [...common, row.label, row.amount, row.share]);
    rows.push([...common, '费用合计', value.costTotal, value.costShare], [...common, value.storage ? '毛利（含仓储费）' : '利润（未含仓储费）', value.profit, value.profitShare]);
    return rows;
  }
  const priorDetail = cxRenderSku;
  cxRenderSku = function () {
    const result = priorDetail.apply(this, arguments), body = cxEl('cxDetailBody'), sku = activeSku();
    if (!sku || !body || !body.querySelector('.pp-section')) return result;
    // 旧利润页整段替换了原财务面板；在最终详情输出之后恢复共同费用视图。
    if (!body.innerHTML.includes('class="cc-cost-composition ')) body.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', render(sku));
    return result;
  };
  root.addEventListener('click', event => {
    const button = event.target.closest('[data-cc-action]');
    if (!button) return;
    const section = button.closest('.cc-cost-composition'), sku = section?.dataset.costsSku || activeSku();
    if (!sku) return;
    event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation();
    const action = button.dataset.ccAction, filters = currentFilters();
    if (action === 'samples') {
      let target = cxEl('cxDetailBody')?.querySelector('.pp-samples-wrap');
      if (!target && cxState.view === 'query') { sqDetailTab = 'finance'; cxRenderSku(); target = cxEl('cxDetailBody')?.querySelector('.pp-samples-wrap'); }
      if (!target) { const tab = cxEl('cxDetailTabs')?.querySelector('[data-pp-tab]'); if (tab) tab.click(); target = cxEl('cxDetailBody')?.querySelector('.pp-samples-wrap'); }
      target?.querySelectorAll('details').forEach(detail => { detail.open = true; });
      target?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start', behavior: 'smooth' }); return;
    }
    if (action === 'export') {
      const value = forSku(sku, filters, ui);
      cxDownloadCsv('SKU费用构成_' + sku + '.csv', ['SKU','采购口径','仓储口径','样本开始','样本结束','样本订单数','平台数','店铺数','样本平台店铺','样本收入USD','费用项目','金额USD','占样本收入'], csvRows(value)); return;
    }
    if (action === 'operating' || action === 'true') ui.basis = action;
    if (action === 'pure') ui.storage = false;
    if (action === 'storage' && forSku(sku, filters, ui).storageComplete) ui.storage = true;
    if (section) section.outerHTML = render(sku, filters, ui); else cxRenderSku();
  }, true);
  root.costCompositionModel = { ready: true, version: '2026.11.18-costs-same-samples', forSku, render, csvRows, currentFilters };
})(window);

/* 销量与利润样本分开核对；销量仍使用当前已验收的同窗、同渠道查询聚合。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  if (root.salesScopeConsistencyModel?.ready) return;
  const query = root.spQueryPeriodModel, sales = root.salesRefreshModel, profitModel = root.ppProfitModel;
  if (!query?.ready || !sales?.ready || !profitModel?.ready) throw Error('销量核对依赖未就绪');
  const finite = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const esc = value => cxEsc(String(value ?? ''));
  const qty = value => finite(value) ? cxQty(value) : '待核';
  const range = (lower, upper) => !finite(lower) ? '待核' : !finite(upper) ? '≥' + qty(lower)
    : lower === upper ? qty(lower) : qty(lower) + '—' + qty(upper);
  const storeName = row => query.normalizeStore(row.platform, row.store);
  const key = row => [row.platform, storeName(row), String(row.orderId || '').replace(/^#/, ''), row.sku].join('|');
  // 旧库存包的salesCoverage截止11/8，不能用于证明11/15无订单。本轮仅使用已完读取数的9店及明确SKU身份。
  const coverageStores = [
    ['Amazon', 'genji furniture-US'], ['Amazon', 'kaguyasu-US'], ['Amazon', 'senyujp-US'], ['Amazon', '中诚-US'],
    ['Shopify', 'AnzhapUS'], ['Shopify', 'KaguyasuUS'], ['Shopify', 'ZimasyaUS'], ['Shopify', 'BizfurnizUS'], ['Wayfair', 'way_linhe']
  ].map(([platform, store]) => ({ platform, store }));
  const audited = sales.meta.netAudit?.platforms || {};
  const latestCoverageVerified = sales.meta.netAudit?.completeOrderThrough === sales.coverage.native.end
    && ['Amazon', 'Shopify', 'Wayfair'].every(platform => audited[platform]?.completeStart <= sales.coverage.native.start
      && audited[platform]?.completeThrough >= sales.coverage.native.end)
    && audited.Amazon?.fullWindowFilterReadbackConfirmed === true
    && audited.Shopify?.returnsCoverage?.allReadComplete === true
    && audited.Wayfair?.storeCoverage?.way_linhe?.completeThrough >= sales.coverage.native.end;
  const identities = new Map(), ownership = new Map();
  for (const [sku, item] of pcCatalog) for (const raw of new Set([sku, ...(item.masterRows || []).map(row => row.rawSku).filter(Boolean)])) {
    if (!ownership.has(raw)) ownership.set(raw, new Set()); ownership.get(raw).add(sku);
  }
  for (const [raw, owners] of ownership) if (owners.size === 1) {
    const canonical = [...owners][0], item = pcCatalog.get(canonical);
    if (item && item.nonProduct !== true) identities.set(raw, canonical);
  }
  function channelCovered(filters) {
    if (!latestCoverageVerified) return false;
    const platforms = sqValues(filters.platform), stores = sqValues(filters.store);
    if (platforms.some(platform => !coverageStores.some(row => row.platform === platform))) return false;
    return stores.every(store => coverageStores.some(row => (!platforms.length || platforms.includes(row.platform)) && sqStoreMatches(row.platform, row.store, store)));
  }
  function zeroCertificate(sku, filters) {
    return identities.has(sku) && channelCovered(filters) && filters.start >= sales.coverage.native.start
      && filters.end <= sales.coverage.native.end && filters.start <= filters.end;
  }
  const priorPeriodCoverage = query.periodCoverage;
  function periodCoverage(filters) {
    const period = priorPeriodCoverage(filters);
    return period.mode !== 'native' || channelCovered(filters) ? period : { ...period, periodComplete: false, hasOverlap: false,
      actualStart: null, actualEnd: null, note: '所选平台／店铺未在本次9店已核净量来源覆盖内；不作0销量。历史参考可单独查询。' };
  }
  query.periodCoverage = periodCoverage;
  const priorSalesFor = query.salesFor;
  function salesFor(sku, filters) {
    const value = priorSalesFor(sku, filters);
    if (value.period.mode === 'native' && !channelCovered(filters)) return { ...value, units: null, unitsUpper: null,
      orders: null, salesAmount: null, salesKnown: false, period: periodCoverage(filters), note: periodCoverage(filters).note };
    if (value.period.mode !== 'native' || value.rows.length) return value;
    const zero = value.period.periodComplete && zeroCertificate(sku, filters);
    const reason = zero ? '本SKU明确库存身份，最新三平台9店全窗已核，无对应订单行，认证净量0'
      : '未取得本SKU在当前渠道及完整期间的最新零销量证据；旧11/8覆盖不替代本次核对';
    return { ...value, units: zero ? 0 : null, unitsUpper: zero ? 0 : null, orders: zero ? 0 : null,
      salesAmount: zero ? 0 : null, salesKnown: zero, zeroCoverageCertified: zero,
      zeroCoverageVersion: zero ? sales.version : null, zeroCanonicalSku: identities.get(sku) || null,
      note: value.note + '；' + reason };
  }
  query.salesFor = salesFor;
  sqSalesValue = function (sku, filters, start = filters.start, end = filters.end) {
    return salesFor(sku, { ...filters, start, end, salesMode: arguments.length > 2 ? 'native' : filters.salesMode || 'auto' });
  };
  const priorBuild = sqBuildRow;
  sqBuildRow = function (sku, filters) {
    const row = priorBuild(sku, filters), selected = salesFor(sku, filters);
    const near = days => salesFor(sku, { ...filters, ...query.nearRange(days), salesMode: 'native' });
    const thirty = near(30), sixty = near(60);
    return { ...row, units: selected.units, unitsUpper: selected.unitsUpper, orders: selected.orders,
      salesAmount: selected.salesAmount, salesKnown: selected.salesKnown, coverageNote: selected.note,
      periodComplete: selected.period.periodComplete, actualCoverageStart: selected.period.actualStart, actualCoverageEnd: selected.period.actualEnd, _period: selected.period,
      _salesRows: selected.rows, net30: thirty.units, net30Upper: thirty.unitsUpper, net60: sixty.units, net60Upper: sixty.unitsUpper,
      zeroCoverageCertified: selected.zeroCoverageCertified || false, zeroCoverageVersion: selected.zeroCoverageVersion || null };
  };
  const priorQuery = sqQuery;
  sqQuery = function (filters, sort, page, size) {
    const result = priorQuery.apply(this, arguments), period = periodCoverage({ ...query.defaults(), ...filters });
    return { ...result, meta: { ...result.meta, period, salesCoverage: period.note, salesBasis: period.label } };
  };
  query.query = sqQuery;
  const priorWindow = sales.skuWindow;
  sales.skuWindow = function (sku, days, mode = 'annual', filters = {}) {
    const value = priorWindow(sku, days, mode, filters);
    if (mode !== 'native') return value;
    const checked = salesFor(sku, { ...query.defaults(), ...filters, ...query.nearRange(days), salesMode: 'native' });
    return { ...value, lower: checked.units, upper: checked.unitsUpper, orderCount: checked.orders,
      zeroFromCompleteCoverage: checked.zeroCoverageCertified || false, zeroCoverageVersion: checked.zeroCoverageVersion || null };
  };
  function currentFilters() {
    if (cxState.view === 'query') return { ...query.defaults(), ...sqUiState.filters };
    const period = srSelectedPeriod(), scope = cfReferenceScope();
    return { ...query.defaults(), ...scope, start: period.start, end: period.end,
      salesMode: sxAnnual() ? 'annual' : 'native', scope: cxState.sales.stockScope || 'business' };
  }
  function context(sku, filters = currentFilters()) {
    const configured = { ...query.defaults(), ...filters }, profit = profitModel.forSku(sku, configured);
    const windows = {
      selected: { filters: configured, label: '所选期间' },
      near30: { filters: { ...configured, ...query.nearRange(30), salesMode: 'native' }, label: '固定近30天' },
      near60: { filters: { ...configured, ...query.nearRange(60), salesMode: 'native' }, label: '固定近60天' }
    };
    for (const value of Object.values(windows)) value.sales = query.salesFor(sku, value.filters);
    const samples = profit.samples || [], sampleKeys = new Set(samples.map(key));
    const rejected = new Map((profit.rejected || []).map(row => [key(row), row]));
    return { sku, filters: configured, windows, profit, sampleKeys, rejected };
  }
  function sampleStatus(order, value) {
    if (value.sampleKeys.has(key(order))) return '已入选当前利润样本';
    const rejected = value.rejected.get(key(order)), reasons = rejected?.missingFields || [];
    return '未入选当前利润样本' + (reasons.length ? '：' + reasons.join('；') : '；不影响此订单按已核证据计入销量');
  }
  function metric(label, value, note) {
    return '<div class="sc-scope-metric"><span>' + esc(label) + '</span><strong>' + esc(value)
      + '</strong><small>' + esc(note) + '</small></div>';
  }
  function html(value, choice = 'selected', opened = false) {
    const selected = value.windows[choice] || value.windows.selected, current = selected.sales;
    const sample = value.profit.selected, allSamples = value.profit.samples || [];
    const included = current.rows.filter(row => value.sampleKeys.has(key(row))).length;
    const mode = current.period.mode, annual = mode === 'annual';
    const orders = [...current.rows].sort((a, b) => String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)) || String(a.orderId).localeCompare(String(b.orderId)));
    const channels = [value.filters.platform || '全部平台', value.filters.store || '全部店铺'].join('／');
    const buttons = Object.entries(value.windows).map(([id, window]) => '<button type="button" data-sc-sales-window="' + id
      + '" aria-pressed="' + (choice === id) + '" class="' + (choice === id ? 'active' : '') + '">'
      + esc(window.label) + '<small>' + esc(window.filters.start + '—' + window.filters.end) + '</small></button>').join('');
    return '<section id="scSalesScopeSummary" class="sc-sales-scope" data-sc-sku="' + esc(value.sku) + '">'
      + '<h3>销量与利润样本核对</h3><p class="sc-scope-caption">' + esc(channels) + '；销量按原订单日。固定近30／60天截止 '
      + esc(sales.coverage.native.end) + '。</p><div class="sc-scope-metrics">'
      + metric('所选期间销量', range(value.windows.selected.sales.units, value.windows.selected.sales.unitsUpper), value.filters.start + '—' + value.filters.end)
      + metric('固定近30天净量', range(value.windows.near30.sales.units, value.windows.near30.sales.unitsUpper), value.windows.near30.filters.start + '—' + value.windows.near30.filters.end)
      + metric('固定近60天净量', range(value.windows.near60.sales.units, value.windows.near60.sales.unitsUpper), value.windows.near60.filters.start + '—' + value.windows.near60.filters.end)
      + metric('利润取样', sample ? sample.sampleCount + '笔' : '无可用自身样本', sample ? sample.periodStart + '—' + sample.periodEnd : '未以其他SKU补值')
      + '</div><p class="sc-scope-explain"><strong>利润表是最近最多20笔可用成本样本，独立于销量日期窗口。</strong>'
      + '样本笔数、样本原数量与已核净销量分别统计。新订单即使暂未取得完整成本，仍按其订单、退款与售后证据计入销量。'
      + (sample ? '当前利润取样覆盖 ' + esc(sample.platformCount + '平台／' + sample.storeCount + '店铺') + '。' : '') + '</p>'
      + '<details class="sc-scope-orders" ' + (opened ? 'open' : '') + '><summary>查看同窗销量订单 · '
      + esc(current.period.label) + ' ' + esc(range(current.units, current.unitsUpper)) + ' · '
      + esc(qty(current.orders)) + '个' + (annual ? '参考' : '有效') + '订单</summary>'
      + '<div class="sc-scope-windows" role="group" aria-label="只切换本销量核对表日期">' + buttons + '</div>'
      + '<p class="sc-scope-caption">切换仅改变本核对表，不更改全局查询日期或最近20笔利润取样。' + esc(current.period.label)
      + ' · ' + esc(selected.filters.start + '—' + selected.filters.end) + '。</p>'
      + '<p class="sc-scope-caption">' + esc(current.note) + '</p>'
      + '<p class="sc-scope-caption">本表 ' + orders.length + '条商品行，其中 ' + included + '条对应当前利润样本（共 '
      + allSamples.length + '笔）；取消／测试行保留便于核对，不计有效订单。退款金额按发生日另列，整套退款有证才扣净量。</p>'
      + '<div class="sc-scope-table">' + cxTable(['订单日期', '平台／店铺', '订单／SKU', '原数量', annual ? '历史参考数量' : '已核净量下界—上界', '订单状态／待核', '与利润样本的对应'], orders.map(order => [
        esc(order.date), esc(order.platform === 'Shopify' ? '独立站' : order.platform) + cxSmall(order.store),
        '<button type="button" class="cx-order-link" data-cx-order="' + esc(cxOrderKey(order)) + '">' + esc(order.orderId) + '</button>' + cxSmall(order.shopSku || order.sku),
        qty(order.quantity), annual ? qty(order.quantity) : range(finite(order.netLower) ? order.netLower : order.net, order.netUpper),
        esc(order.status || '状态未核') + cxSmall(order.pendingReason || ''), esc(sampleStatus(order, value))
      ])) + '</div>' + (!orders.length ? '<p class="sc-scope-caption">本窗口未见商品行；是否可判0以覆盖与SKU计量证据为准，不以空表直接补0。</p>' : '')
      + '</details></section>';
  }
  const priorDetail = cxRenderSku;
  cxRenderSku = function () {
    const result = priorDetail.apply(this, arguments), body = cxEl('cxDetailBody');
    const sku = cxState.detail?.sku || (cxState.view === 'query' ? sqDetailRow?.sku : '');
    if (!sku || !body) return result;
    // 原详情中的销量与订单页保留；在摘要/利润处补同窗核对入口，避免把利润样本当销量明细。
    const querySummary = cxState.view === 'query' && ['overview', 'finance'].includes(sqDetailTab);
    const salesSummary = cxState.view !== 'query' && cxState.detail?.tab === 'overview';
    const profitVisible = body.querySelector('.pp-samples-wrap');
    if (!querySummary && !salesSummary && !profitVisible) return result;
    for (const node of body.querySelectorAll('.sc-sales-scope')) node.remove();
    body.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', html(context(sku)));
    return result;
  };
  root.addEventListener('click', event => {
    const button = event.target.closest('[data-sc-sales-window]');
    if (!button) return;
    const host = button.closest('.sc-sales-scope'), sku = host?.dataset.scSku;
    if (!sku || !['selected', 'near30', 'near60'].includes(button.dataset.scSalesWindow)) return;
    event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation();
    const holder = document.createElement('div');
    holder.innerHTML = html(context(sku), button.dataset.scSalesWindow, true);
    host.replaceWith(holder.firstElementChild);
  }, true);
  function refreshMarkdown() {
    const model = root.markdownFollowupModel;
    if (!model?.ready) return;
    const range30 = query.nearRange(30), range60 = query.nearRange(60);
    model.meta.strategySalesThrough = model.meta.strategySalesThrough || model.meta.salesThrough;
    Object.assign(model.meta, { salesThrough: sales.coverage.native.end,
      window30: range30.start + '—' + range30.end, window60: range60.start + '—' + range60.end });
    for (const row of model.rows) {
      const filters = { ...query.defaults(), platform: '', store: '', scope: 'business' };
      const thirty = salesFor(row.sku, { ...filters, ...query.nearRange(30), salesMode: 'native' });
      const sixty = salesFor(row.sku, { ...filters, ...query.nearRange(60), salesMode: 'native' });
      const allNative = salesFor(row.sku, { ...filters, start: sales.coverage.native.start, end: sales.coverage.native.end, salesMode: 'native' });
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(row, 'strategyLastValidOrderDate')) row.strategyLastValidOrderDate = row.lastValidOrderDate;
      const previous = row.strategyLastValidOrderDate;
      const lastNative = allNative.rows.filter(item => finite(item.netLower) && item.netLower > 0).map(item => item.date).filter(Boolean).sort().at(-1);
      row.lastValidOrderDate = lastNative || (previous && previous < sales.coverage.native.start ? previous : null);
      row.lastValidOrderBasis = lastNative ? '最新完整原生核对窗内正净量订单，截止' + sales.coverage.native.end : '完整核对窗内无正净量订单；更早历史日期按原有依据单列';
      Object.assign(row, { net30Lower: thirty.units, net30Upper: thirty.unitsUpper, net60Lower: sixty.units, net60Upper: sixty.unitsUpper,
        valid30: thirty.orders, salesCoverageStatus: thirty.note, salesCoverageVersion: sales.version });
      if (!finite(thirty.units)) { row.remainingDays = null; row.selloutGroup = '销量待核，不能据此判断零动销'; }
    }
    const node = document.getElementById('markdownReviewData');
    if (node?.textContent) {
      const packet = JSON.parse(node.textContent), bySku = new Map(model.rows.map(row => [row.sku, row]));
      for (const row of packet.rows || []) { const current = bySku.get(row.sku); if (current) for (const field of ['net30Lower', 'net30Upper', 'net60Lower', 'net60Upper', 'valid30', 'lastValidOrderDate', 'strategyLastValidOrderDate', 'lastValidOrderBasis', 'salesCoverageStatus', 'salesCoverageVersion', 'remainingDays', 'selloutGroup']) row[field] = current[field]; }
      Object.assign(packet.meta, { salesThrough: model.meta.salesThrough, window30: model.meta.window30, window60: model.meta.window60, strategySalesThrough: model.meta.strategySalesThrough });
      packet.meta.salesCoverageCertificate = '本SKU身份＋最新三平台9店完整订单窗；非旧库存salesCoverage';
      node.textContent = JSON.stringify(packet);
    }
    root.inventoryConsistencyModel?.refreshMarkdown(); root.inventoryConsistencyModel?.decorateMarkdown();
    model.render();
  }
  query.clearCaches();
  root.salesScopeConsistencyModel = { ready: true, version: '20261118-sales-scope', context, html, currentFilters,
    selectedSales: salesFor, sampleStatus, zeroCertificate, latestCoverageVerified, coverageStores, identities, refreshMarkdown };
  refreshMarkdown();
  const marker = document.createElement('span'); marker.id = 'salesScopeConsistencyReady'; marker.hidden = true; document.body.appendChild(marker);
})(window);

/* 导航出口统一可见模块与选中状态，避免旧覆写链留下上一页。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const routes = [
    ['plans', 'planPane', 'showPlans'],
    ['warehouses', 'warehousePane', 'showWarehouses'],
    ['audit', 'auditPane', 'showAudit'],
    ['pieces', 'piecesPane', 'showPieces'],
    ['sales', 'cxSalesPane', 'cxShowSales'],
    ['refunds', 'raPane', 'raShowRefunds'],
    ['query', 'sqQueryPane', 'sqShowQuery'],
    ['replenishment', 'cxPlansPane', 'cxShowPlans'],
    ['newproducts', 'cxNewsPane', 'cxShowNews']
  ];
  const priorView = setPageView;
  function sync(view) {
    if (!routes.some(([key]) => key === view)) return;
    cxState.view = view; pageState.view = view;
    for (const [key, paneId, buttonId] of routes) {
      const pane = document.getElementById(paneId), button = document.getElementById(buttonId), active = key === view;
      if (pane) pane.hidden = !active;
      if (button) { button.classList.toggle('active', active); button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(active)); }
    }
  }
  setPageView = function (view) {
    const result = priorView.apply(this, arguments);
    sync(view);
    return result;
  };
  root.dashboardNavigationModel = {ready: true, routes, sync};
  sync(cxState.view);
})(window);

(function(){
 if(!window.inventoryConsistencyModel?.ready||!window.costCompositionModel?.ready||!window.salesScopeConsistencyModel?.ready||!window.dashboardNavigationModel?.ready)throw Error("一致性修复未完成初始化");
 const marker=document.createElement("span");marker.id="dashboardConsistencyReady";marker.hidden=true;document.body.appendChild(marker);
})();

</script>
